																					lcEdt + lcGrD + lcCtD, lcEDNs, pCidC, ;
																					lcEmpDs + csDestinos.DopeDs + Str(lnNumes, 6), ;
																					pSeq, CsDesEestI.Pesos)

																If Not llCopBarra Or (lnQtd > 1) && Não Copiar o Barra
																	llLcb = .t.
																	If llMntBarra And loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).CursorQuery('SigOpEtq', 'LocalEti', 'CBars', ThisForm.CBars)
																		llLcb = .f.
																	EndIf
																	If llLcb
																		Replace CodBarras With 0 In DesHis
																	EndIf
																EndIf 

																If llRecalcs
																	=SqlCalcP2(DesHis.Emps, DesHis.Grupos, DesHis.Estos, DesHis.CPros, DesHis.Datas, DesHis.CodCors, DesHis.CodTams, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
																	=fRecalculaC(DesHis.Emps, DesHis.CPros, DesHis.Datas, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
																EndIf
															EndIf 
														EndIf 
													EndIf 

													*** Tiago - 01/10/2013 - Ícaro - Processo de cópia da produção
													**********************************************************************************************
													If llTaOK And csDestinos.Globalizas = 3
														oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... PCP...'

														lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpPic Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' And CItens = ] + Str(CsDesEestI.CItens, 4) + [ Order By cIdChaves]
														If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crOpPic') < 1)
															=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crOpPic)')
															Return 0
														EndIf

														If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('crOpPic') > 0)
															Select crOpPic
															Scan
																Select crOpPic
																Scatter Memvar Memo

																* Lança no Destino
																Select DesOpPic
																Append Blank
																Gather Memvar Memo
																
																Replace Cpros	   With csDesEestI.Cpros, ;
																		Dpros	   With Iif(llCopDesIt, DesOpPic.DPros, csDesEestI.DPros), ;
																		EmpDs	   With lcEmpDs, ;
																		Emps       With lcEmpDs, ;
																		Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;
																		Numes      With lnNumes, ;
																		EmpDopNums With lcEmpDs + csDestinos.DopeDs + Str(lnNumes, 6), ;
																		EmpDNps    With lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10), ;
																		EmpDopNops With lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Nops,10), ;
																		CodBarras  With 0 , ;
																		cIdChaves  With fUniqueIds() In DesOpPic
																		
																lnNop = crOpPic.Nops
															Endscan

															*** Checa no destino e no cursor se já não tem a movimentação lançada, pois outro pedido já pode ter sido copiado com a mesma OP
															loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).CursorQuery('SigPdMvf', 'TmpDesMvf', 'EmpDNps', lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10))
															Select DesPdMvf
															=Seek(lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10),'DesPdMvf','EmpDNps')
															If Eof('TmpDesMvf') And Eof('DesPdMvf')					
																lcQuery = [Select top 1 * From SigPdMvf Where Nops = ?lnNop Order By cIdChaves]
																If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crPdMvf') < 1)
																	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crPdMvf)')
																	Return 0
																Endif
																
																Select crPdMvf
																Scatter Memvar Memo
																m.CodPds = csDesEestI.Cpros
																m.Emps = lcEmpDs
																m.EmpDNps = lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10)
																m.cIdChaves = fUniqueIds()												

																* Lança no Destino
																Select DesPdMvf
																Append Blank
																Gather Memvar Memo	

																lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdNec Where EmpDNps = ?crPdMvf.EmpDNps Order By cIdChaves]															
																If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crCdNec') < 1)
																	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crCdNec)')
																	Return 0
																Endif

																Select crCdNec
																Scatter Memvar Memo
																m.Emps = lcEmpDs
																m.EmpDNps = lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10)
																m.ChkSubn = 0
																m.cIdChaves = fUniqueIds()																																																										

																* Lança no Destino
																Select DesCdNec
																Append Blank
																Gather Memvar Memo																

																lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdNei Where EmpDNps = ?crPdMvf.EmpDNps Order By cIdChaves]															
																If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crCdNei') < 1)
																	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crCdNei)')
																	Return 0
																Endif

																Select crCdNei
																Scan
																	Scatter Memvar Memo
																	m.Emps = lcEmpDs
																	m.EmpDNps = lcEmpDs + m.Dopps + Str(m.Numps,10)	
																	m.cIdChaves = fUniqueIds()																																										
																	
																	* Lança no Destino
																	Select DesCdNei
																	Append Blank
																	Gather Memvar Memo
																Endscan																
															Endif 																																								
														EndIf 																		
													Else
														thisform.Cpros = CsDesEestI.Cpros
														thisform.lancaproducao([O])
													Endif
													Select CsDesEestI													
													**********************************************************************************************																					 													
												EndScan

												Replace ValInis With lnTot, ;
														Valos   With lnTot In DesEest

												lcNfa = DesEest.Notas

												Select DesNfis
												Go Top In DesNfis
												If Not Eof([DesNfis]) And llImpostos
													* Calculando os Impostos
													lnFNt = DesNfis.VlFretes
													lnSNt = DesNfis.VlSeguros
													lcCfi = DesNfis.CFis
													=fCalcularImpostos(0, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1), [DesEest], [DesEestI], .f., [DesNfis], lnFNt, lnSNt, lcCfi, .f., [DesPro])
												EndIf
																							
												Select DesEestI
												Sum Totas To lnTot

												Update DesEestI ;
												   Set ChkSubn = .f., ;
												   	   QtBaixas = 0
																							
												Replace ValInis With lnTot, ;
														Valos   With lnTot, ;
														Notas	With lcNfa In DesEest 

												Select DesNfis
												Go Top In DesNfis
												If Not Eof([DesNfis]) And Not Empty(DesNFis.TotNotas) And (DesNFis.TotNotas <> DesEest.Valos)
													Replace Valos With DesNFis.TotNotas In DesEest
												EndIf

												oInfer.Complete
											EndIf
											
											Use In csDesEestI
											If llCorTams
												Use In csDesEstI2
											EndIf
											**********************************************************************************************
										EndIf
									EndIf && If Not llSemItens
								Else
									=Messagebox('O Arquivo de Geração Do Número Sequêncial de Lançamento de Operações Não Foi Encontrado!!!' + ;
												Chr(13) + 'A Operação "' + Alltrim(csDestinos.Dopes) + '" Não Será Lançada!!! Verifique!!!', ;

								Endif

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigMvPar
								If llTaOK
									oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Parcelas...'
									lcQuery = [Select *, 0000000 as nOperOrig ] + ;
											    [From SigMvPar ] + ;
											   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;

										=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPar)')
										Return 0
									EndIf
								EndIf

								If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('csPar') > 0)
									lnVpg = 0
									Select csPar
									Scan
										Select csPar
										Scatter Memvar Memo
										If Seek(m.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
											lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
										Else
											lnNoper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr', loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
											If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
												=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 4)')
												Return 0

											Insert Into LocalCopNp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
													(csPar.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsPar.EmpDopNums)

											Insert Into CrSigPcOnp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
													(csPar.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsPar.EmpDopNums)

										Endif

										* Lança no Destino
										Select DesPar
										Append Blank
										Gather Memvar Memo
										
										lnVpg  = lnVpg + (DesPar.Valos * DesPar.CotFpgs)
										lcEDNs = lcEmpDs + csDestinos.DopeDs + Str(lnNumes, 6)
										Select DesPar
										Replace Emps       With lcEmpDs, ;
												Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;

												Pagos 	   With Iif(Valos=Vpags,'0','1'), ;

										If (lnMntPagos = 2)
											Replace Pagos With [1], ;

										EndIf

										If Not Empty(DesPar.Moefpgs) And ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesPar.Moefpgs, 'A Moeda')
											ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMoe.Moeqs,  'A Moeda')
										EndIf
										
										Replace nOperOrig With lnNoper in CsPar
									EndScan
									
									Replace Valos With lnVpg In DesEest
								EndIf 
								**********************************************************************************************

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigMvMov
								oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Valor Informado...'
								lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
										    [From SigMvMov ] + ;
										   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;

										Append Blank

										Select DesEstMo

												Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;

										If Not Empty(DesEstMo.Moeds) And ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesEstMo.Moeds, 'A Moeda')

									EndScan

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigCdCmm
								oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Comissões...'
								lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;

										Select DesComis

									EndScan
								EndIf 
								**********************************************************************************************

								**********************************************************************************************
								lnVOper = 0
								If llCopMccrs
									oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Conta Corrente...'

									* Lançando o SigMvCcr
									lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
												[From SigMvCcr ] + ;
											   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;

									If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csMccr') < 1)
										=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csMccr)')
										Return 0
									EndIf
									 
									If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('csMccr') > 0)
										Go Top In csMccr
										*** Tiago - 03/05/2016 - Comentei pois estava gerando o mesmo NUMCS para as parcelas diferentes e tem que gerar um número para cada
*!*											lnNumcs = fGerUniqueKey(csMccr.Dopcs, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
										lnNumcOri = 0
										lnNumcs = 0
										Select csMccr

											*** Tiago - 03/05/2016 - Incluído checagem para gerar NUMCS no destino para cada NUMCS da origem
											If csMccr.Numcs <> lnNumcOri
												lnNumcs = fGerUniqueKey(csMccr.Dopcs, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
												lnNumcOri = csMccr.Numcs											
											Endif

											Select DesMccr
											Append Blank
											Gather Memvar Memo
											
											If Used('Cspar')
												Select cspar
												Locate For Nopers = m.Nopers
												If Not Eof()
													lnNoper = csPar.nOperOrig
												Else
													If Seek(CsMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
														lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews

														Insert Into LocalCopNp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;

																(csmccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsMccr.EmpDopNums)
													Endif
													Insert into csPar (nOpers, nOperOrig ) Values (CsMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
												EndIf

												Endif

												Insert Into csPar (Nopers, nOperOrig ) Values (csMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
											Endif

											lnVOper = lcEmpDs + Alltrim(Str(lnNoper, 9))
											&&lnNumcs = 0

											If Not Used([crDestParac])

												Return 0

											If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)

												Return 0

												If Not Empty(lcRet)

												If Not Empty(lcRet)

											EndIf

													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMoe.Moeqs,  'A Moeda')

											=fRecalculaS( Grupos,  Contas,  Datas, Moedas,  loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))

											=fRecalculaS( SGrupos, SContas, Datas, SMoedas, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))

										   [Order By EmpDopNums, cIdChaves]

									=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (cspgCli)')
									Return 0
								EndIf

								If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('csPgCli') > 0)
									Select csPgCli
									Scan
										Select csPgCli
										Scatter Memvar Memo

										* Lança no Destino
										Select DesPgcli
										Append Blank
										Gather Memvar Memo
										
										Select DesPgCli
										Replace Emps       With lcEmpDs, ;
												Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;
												Numes      With lnNumes, ;
												EmpDopNums With lcEDNs, ;
												cIdChaves  With fUniqueIds() In DesPgCli

										lcRet = ThisForm.LancaClientes(DesPgCli.Contas)
										If Not Empty(lcRet)
											lcConOs = lcRet
											Replace Contas With lcConOs In DesPgCli
										EndIf
									EndScan
								EndIf 
								**********************************************************************************************

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigPrTtl
								oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Lancamento Recebimento...'
								lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
										    [From SigPrTtl ] + ;
										   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;
										   [Order By EmpDopNums, cIdChaves]

								If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csPrTot') < 1)
									=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPrTot)')
									Return 0
								EndIf

								If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('csPrTot') > 0)
									Select csPrTot
									Scan
										Select csPrTot
										Scatter Memvar Memo

										* Lança no Destino
										Select DesPrTot
										Append Blank
										Gather Memvar Memo
										
										Select DesPrTot
										Replace Emps       With lcEmpDs, ;
												Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;
												Numes      With lnNumes, ;
												EmpDopNums With lcEDNs, ;
												cIdChaves  With fUniqueIds() In DesPrTot
									EndScan
								EndIf 
								**********************************************************************************************

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigCdPit
								oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Lancamento Recebimento...'
								lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
										    [From SigCdPit ] + ;
										   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;
										   [Order By EmpDopNums, cIdChaves]

								If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csPrit') < 1)
									=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPrit)')
									Return 0
								EndIf

								If llTaOK And llEmpOk And (RecCount('csPrit') > 0)
									If llCopMccrs
										Select csPrit
										Scan
											lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
													    [From SigMvCcr ] + ;
													   [Where Nopers = ] + Str(CsPrit.NOpers)+ [ ] + ;
													   [Order By EmpDopNums, cIdChaves]

											If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csMccr') < 1)
												=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csMccr)')
												Return 0
											EndIf

											Go Top In CsMccr
											lnNumcs = fGerUniqueKey(CsMccr.Dopcs, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)

											Select CsMccr
											Scan
												lnNoper = CsMccr.Nopers
												If Seek(CsMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
													lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
													If Seek(Str(lnNoper,7)+CsMccr.Opers,'DesMccr','Nopers') Or Seek(Str(lnNoper,7)+CsMccr.Opers,'DesPMccr','Nopers')
														Exit
													EndIf
													lcSql = [Select cidchaves,grupos,Contas,Datas,Moedas,sgrupos,scontas,smoedas From SigMvCcr Where Nopers = ]+Str(lnNoper)
													If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalMccr') ) < 1
														=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalMccr)')
														Return 0
													EndIf
													Select LocalMccr
													Scan
														=fRecalculaS( LocalMccr.Grupos,  LocalMccr.Contas,  LocalMccr.Datas, LocalMccr.Moedas,  loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
														=fRecalculaS( LocalMccr.SGrupos, LocalMccr.SContas, LocalMccr.Datas, LocalMccr.SMoedas, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))

														lcSql = [delete From SigMvCcr Where cidchaves = ']+LocalMccr.Cidchaves+[']
														If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).sqlexecute(lcSql,' ') ) < 1
															=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (delete From SigMvCcr)')
															Return 0
														EndIf
													EndScan
												Endif

												Select CsMccr
												Scatter Memvar Memo

												* Lança no Destino
												Select DesMccr
												Append Blank
												Gather Memvar Memo
												
												If Used('Cspar')
													Select cspar
													Locate For Nopers = m.Nopers
													If Not Eof()
														lnNoper = csPar.nOperOrig
													Else
														If Seek(CsMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
															lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
														Else
															lnNoper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr', loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
															If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
																=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 4)')
																Return 0
															EndIf
															Insert Into LocalCopNp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																	(csMccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsMccr.EmpDopNums)

															Insert Into CrSigPcOnp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																	(csmccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsMccr.EmpDopNums)
														Endif
														Insert into csPar (nOpers, nOperOrig ) Values (CsMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
													EndIf
												Else
													If Seek(CsMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
														lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
													Else
														lnNoper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr', loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
														If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
															=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 4)')
															Return 0
														EndIf
														Insert Into LocalCopNp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																(csMccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsMccr.EmpDopNums)

														Insert Into CrSigPcOnp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																(csmccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CsMccr.EmpDopNums)
													Endif
													Create Cursor cspar (Nopers n(9), nOperOrig n(9))
													Insert Into csPar (Nopers, nOperOrig ) Values (csMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
												Endif

												lnVOper = lcEmpDs + Alltrim(Str(lnNoper, 9))
												&&lnNumcs = 0
												
												loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).CursorQuery([SigCdPac], [crDestParac], .f., .f., [SigKeys])
												If Not Used([crDestParac])
													=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - crDestParac)])
													Return 0
												EndIf

												pCidC = Dtos(ldDatas)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDatas), loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn),"@L 999999")+crDestParac.SigKeys
												If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
													=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 5)')
													Return 0
												EndIf
												
												Select DesMccr
												Replace DatAlts    With Datetime(),;
														Nopers     With lnNoper, ;
														VOpers	   With lnVOper, ;
														cIdChaves  With pCidC In DesMccr

												If Not Empty(DesMccr.GrupAges)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesMccr.GrupAges, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.GrupEms) And (DesMccr.GrupAges <> DesMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesMccr.GrupEms, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.Grupos) And Not InList(DesMccr.Grupos, DesMccr.GrupAges, DesMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesMccr.Grupos, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.SGrupos) And Not InList(DesMccr.SGrupos, DesMccr.Grupos, DesMccr.GrupAges, DesMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesMccr.SGrupos, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf

												If Not Empty(DesMccr.ContAges)
													lcRet = ThisForm.LancaClientes(DesMccr.ContAges)
													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace ContAges With lcRet In DesMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.Contas) &&And (DesMccr.ContAges <> DesMccr.Contas)
													lcRet = ThisForm.LancaClientes(DesMccr.Contas)
													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace Contas With lcRet In DesMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.ContEms) &&And Not InList(DesMccr.ContEms, DesMccr.ContAges, DesMccr.Contas)
													lcRet = ThisForm.LancaClientes(DesMccr.ContEms)
													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace ContEms With lcRet In DesMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.SContas) &&And Not InList(DesMccr.SContas, DesMccr.ContEms, DesMccr.ContAges, DesMccr.Contas)
													lcRet = ThisForm.LancaClientes(DesMccr.SContas)
													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace SContas With lcRet In DesMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf

												If Not Empty(DesMccr.Moedas) And ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMccr.Moedas, 'A Moeda')
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMoe.Moeqs,  'A Moeda')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesMccr.SMoedas) And (DesMccr.SMoedas <> DesMccr.Moedas)
													If Not Empty(DesMccr.SMoedas) And ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMccr.SMoedas, 'A Moeda')
														ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMoe.Moeqs,  'A Moeda')
													EndIf
												EndIf
												
												Select DesMccr
												=fRecalculaS( Grupos,  Contas,  Datas, Moedas,  loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
												=fRecalculaS( SGrupos, SContas, Datas, SMoedas, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
											EndScan

											* Lançando o SigMvCpv
											lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
														[From SigMvCpv ] + ;
													   [Where Nopers = ] + Str(CsPrit.NOpers)+ [ ] + ;
													   [Order By cIdChaves, Nopers, EmpDopNums]

											If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csPMccr') < 1)
												=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPMccr)')
												Return 0
											EndIf

											Go Top In csPMccr
											lnNumcs = fGerUniqueKey(csPMccr.Dopcs, loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)											 

											Select csPMccr
											Scan
												Scatter Memvar Memo

												If Seek(CspMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
													lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
													If Seek(Str(lnNoper,7)+CsMccr.Opers,'DesMccr','Nopers') Or Seek(str(lnNoper,7)+CsMccr.Opers,'DesPMccr','Nopers')
														Exit
													EndIf
													lcSql = [Delete From SigMvCpv Where Nopers = ]+Str(lnNoper)
													If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).sqlexecute(lcSql,' ') ) < 1
														=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete SigMvCpv)')
														Return 0
													EndIf
												Endif

												Select DesPMccr
												Append Blank

															lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews

															If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)

																Return 0

																	(cspMccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CspMccr.EmpDopNums)

																	(cspmccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CspMccr.EmpDopNums)
														Endif
														Insert into csPar (nOpers, nOperOrig ) Values (CspMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
													EndIf
												Else
													If Seek(CspMccr.Nopers,'LocalCopNp','Nopers')
														lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
													Else
														lnNoper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr', loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).pnIdConn)
														If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
															=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 4)')
															Return 0
														EndIf
														Insert Into LocalCopNp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																(cspMccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CspMccr.EmpDopNums)

														Insert Into CrSigPcOnp (NoperOrigs, nOperNews, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) Values ;
																(cspmccr.Nopers,lnNoper,fUniqueIds(),CspMccr.EmpDopNums)
													Endif
													Create Cursor cspar (Nopers n(9), nOperOrig n(9))
													Insert Into csPar (Nopers, nOperOrig ) Values (cspMccr.Nopers, lnNoper)
												Endif

												lnVOper = lcEmpDs + Alltrim(Str(lnNoper, 9))
												&&lnNumcs = 0
												
												loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1).CursorQuery([SigCdPac], [crDestParac], .f., .f., [SigKeys])
												If Not Used([crDestParac])
													=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - crDestParac)])
													Return 0
												EndIf

												If (loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 2).Commit() < 1)
													=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - Commit - fGerUniqueKey 5)')
													Return 0

												Replace DatAlts    With Datetime(), ;
														Nopers     With lnNoper, ;
														VOpers	   With lnVOper, ;

												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.GrupEms) And (DesPMccr.GrupAges <> DesPMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesPMccr.GrupEms, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.Grupos) And Not InList(DesPMccr.Grupos, DesPMccr.GrupAges, DesPMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesPMccr.Grupos, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.SGrupos) And Not InList(DesPMccr.SGrupos, DesPMccr.Grupos, DesPMccr.GrupAges, DesPMccr.GrupEms)
													ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdGcr', 'DesGccr', 'Codigos', DesPMccr.SGrupos, 'O Grupo')
												EndIf

													If Not Empty(lcRet)

													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.Contas) &&And (DesPMccr.ContAges <> DesPMccr.Contas)

													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace Contas With lcRet In DesPMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.ContEms) &&And Not InList(DesPMccr.ContEms, DesPMccr.ContAges, DesPMccr.Contas)

													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace ContEms With lcRet In DesPMccr
													EndIf
												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.SContas) &&And Not InList(DesPMccr.SContas, DesPMccr.ContEms, DesPMccr.ContAges, DesPMccr.Contas)

													If Not Empty(lcRet)
														Replace SContas With lcRet In DesPMccr

												EndIf
												If Not Empty(DesPMccr.SMoedas) And (DesPMccr.SMoedas <> DesPMccr.Moedas)
													If Not Empty(DesPMccr.SMoedas) And ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesPMccr.SMoedas, 'A Moeda')
														ThisForm.LancaDestino('SigCdMoe', 'DesMoe', 'CMoes', DesMoe.Moeqs,  'A Moeda')
													EndIf
												EndIf
											EndScan
										EndScan
									EndIf
									Select csPrit
									Scan
										Select csPrit
										Scatter Memvar Memo

										Select DesPrit
										Append Blank
										Gather Memvar Memo

											lnNoper = LocalCopNp.NoperNews
										Endif
										
										Select DesPrit
										Replace Emps       With lcEmpDs, ;
												Dopes      With csDestinos.DopeDs, ;

										If Not Empty(lcRet)
											lcConOs = lcRet
											Replace Contas With lcConOs In DesPrit
										EndIf
									EndScan
								EndIf 
								**********************************************************************************************

								**********************************************************************************************
								* Lançando o SigCdPbx
								oSuper.Rodape.Caption = 'Processando... Baixa de Recebimento...'
								lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
										    [From SigCdPbx ] + ;
										   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [' ] + ;
										   [Order By EmpDopNums, cIdChaves]

								If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csPrBx') < 1)
									=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPrBx)')
									Return 0
								EndIf

										Select DesPrBx

									EndScan

										    [From SigCdPgr ] + ;

										Select DesPgRec

									EndScan

*!*									thisform.lancaproducao([F])

												lcGruOs = lcGrOsE

												lcGruOs = DesOpe.GruOrigs

												lcConOs = DesDelet.ContaOs

												lcConDs = DesOpe.ConDests
										   Case lnGruConDs = 4 && Operação da Origem

				If llTransOk1
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigExMvc Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigExMvc)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigMvCab)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigMvItn Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigMvItn)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigMvPar Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigMvPar)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigMvMov Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigMvMov)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf
				
				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdCmm Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdCmm)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdPbx Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdPbx)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdCar Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdCar)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdCtt Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdCtt)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdCrd Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdCrd)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdPgc Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdPgc)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigPrTtl Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigPrTtl)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdPit Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdPit)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If llTransOk1 
					lcQuery = [Delete From SigCdPgr Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcChave + [']
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigCdPgr)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif

				If Not llTransOk1
					=MessageBox('Problemas com a Operação : ' + Chr(13) + ;
								'Empresa : ' + pEmp + ' - Operação : ' + Alltrim(pDop) + ;
								' - Código : ' + Alltrim(Str(pNum)) + Chr(13) + ;
								'Contate o Suporte Técnico Com os Códigos Acima!!!', ;
								16, 'Erro na Deleção!!!')
				EndIf
			EndScan

			If llTransOk1
				If Not fRecalculaS( .t., loConexao(ThisForm.lnConexao, 1))
					llTransOk1 = .f.
				Endif
			EndIf
		EndIf

		If llTransOk1
			If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.Commit() < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Commit - 2)')
				ThisForm.poDataMgr.RollBack()		
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Endif	
		If llImp
			If (Reccount('csImpressao') > 0)
				Select csImpressao
				Go Top

				Do Form SigMvDoc With 'SigMvDop', 'Impressão da Transferência'
			EndIf

			If .f.
				Report Form SigMvDop && Linha Colocada Apenas Para Compilação
			Endif
		EndIf
		
		=Messagebox('Cópia Concluída!!!', 0+48, '')
		
		ThisForm.LimpaSelecao()
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmdSelecionar.Click(.t.)
	EndIf
EndIf

Return 1
* Fim ********************************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE selecionar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE selecionar
Private pDtI, pDtF, pNot, pTru
Local lcEmp, lcOpe, ldDtI, ldDtF, lcPro, llNPr, lcArq, llAchouDes

pNot = .f.
pTru = .t.

lcPro = Padr(ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.ListItem(ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.ListIndex), 20)
llNPr = (ThisForm.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Value = 1) && 1-Não Processados; 2-Já Processados

If Not llNPr && Se For Reprocessar as Operações
	If Messagebox('Confirma a Seleção de Operações Já Processadas Anteriormente?' + Chr(13) + ;
				  'Este Processo Poderá Lançar Registros Duplicados no Destino no ' + Chr(13) + ;
				  'Caso dos Mesmos Não Terem Sido Anteriormente Excluídos!!!', 4+32+256, 'Atenção!!!') <> 6
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Value = 1
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigPcOop], [crSigPcOop], [Processos], lcPro)
If Eof([crSigPcOop])
	=MessageBox([O Processo ] + lcPro + [ Não Foi Encontrado!!!], 16, [(Selecionar - crSigPcOop)])
	Return 0
EndIf

ThisForm.cntComplemento.getToleras.Value = crSigPcOop.Toleras

If (crSigPcOop.Fiscal = 1) && Copiar Arquivos Fiscais
	If Not Empty(ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Value)
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Value = ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Value - 5
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Refresh
	EndIf
EndIf

* Apanha as Operações de Origem Que Servirão de Filtro Para Apanhar as Operações a Serem Processadas no SigMvCab
lcQuery = [Select Distinct Emps, Dopes, Codigos, Situas, Delets, cIdChaves, Globalizas ] + ;
  			[From SigPcOoI ] + ;
  		   [Where Processos = '] + lcPro + [' ] + ;
  		   [Order By Emps, Dopes, Codigos, Situas]

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csCpOI') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csCpOI)')
	Return 0
EndIf

* Cria Um Cursor Em Branco Para Adicionar as Operações a Serem Processadas do SigMvCab (Para a Grade)
Create Cursor csDados (Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Datas d(8), Valos n(12, 2), Notas c(6), ;
					   Usuars c(10), ContaOs c(10), ContaDs c(10), EmpDs c(3), Deletado l(1))
Index On Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) Tag EmpDopNums

lcDes = Padr(ThisForm.cntFiltro.getEmpDs.Value, 3)
ldDtI = ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Value
ldDtF = ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.Value

If Empty(ldDtI)
	=Messagebox('Data Inicial Inválida!!!', 0+48, '')
	ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.SetFocus
	Return 0
Endif

If Empty(ldDtF)
	=Messagebox('Data Final Inválida!!!', 0+48, '')
	ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.SetFocus
	Return 0
Endif

If (ldDtF < ldDtI)
	=Messagebox('A Data Final Não Pode Ser Menor Que a Inicial!!!', 0+48, '')
	ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.SetFocus
	Return 0
Endif

Select csCpOI
oProg = CreateObject('fwprogressbar', 'Analisando Operações Para Processamento...', Recc())
oProg.Show
Scan
	oProg.Update(.t.)

	lcEmp = Iif(Empty(ThisForm.cntFiltro.getEmpOs.Value), csCpOI.Emps, Padr(ThisForm.cntFiltro.getEmpOs.Value, 3))
	lcOpe = csCpOI.Dopes
	lnChk = csCpOI.Situas     && 1-Todas; 2-Pendentes; 3-Baixadas (0 => 1-Default)
	lnDoc = crSigPcOop.Notas  && 1-Sim; 2-Não; 3-Ambos (0 => 3-Default)
	lnOrd = crSigPcOop.Ordems && 1-Operação; 2-Data; 3-Nota; 4-Origem; 5-Destino (0 => 1-Default)
	lnProd = csCpOI.Globalizas && 1-Todas; 2-Pendentes; 3-Industrializadas; 4-Finalizadas
	lcGru = crSigPcOop.Grupo  && Grupo Empresarial
	lcCla = crSigPcOop.Class  && Calssificação Empresarial
	llDel = (csCpOI.Delets == 1)

	llAchouDes = .t.

	If Empty(lcOpe)
		=Messagebox('Operação Inválida!!!', 0+48, '')
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.SetFocus
		Return 0
	Endif

	Set Talk On
	Set Talk Window
	******************************************************************************************************************
	pDtI = fDtoSQL(ldDtI)
	pDtF = fDtoSQL(ldDtF, '23:59:59')

	lcQuery = [Select a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, ] + Iif(lnChk = 3, [a.DtBaixas], [a.Datas]) + [ as Datas, ] + ;
					 [a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Usuars, a.ContaOs, a.ContaDs, a.EmpDs ] + ;
				[From SigMvCab a, SigCdEmp b ] + ;
			   [Where ] + Iif(lnChk = 3, [a.DtBaixas], [a.Datas]) + [ BetWeen ?pDtI And ?pDtF And ] + ;
			   		 [a.Dopes = '] + lcOpe + [' And ] + ;
			   		 Iif(Empty(lcEmp), [], [a.Emps = ']  + lcEmp + [' And ]) + ;
			   		 Iif(Empty(lcDes), [], [a.EmpDs = '] + lcDes + [' And ]) + ;
			   		 Iif(lnDoc=1, [Not a.Notas = '      ' And ], Iif(lnDoc=2, [a.Notas = '      ' And ], [])) + ;
			   		 Iif(lnChk<2, [], Iif(lnChk=2, [a.ChkSubn = ?pNot And ], [a.ChkSubn = ?pTru And ])) + ;
			   		 Iif(lnProd<2, [], Iif(lnProd=2, [a.Nops = 0 And ],Iif(lnProd=3, [a.Nops <> 0 And ],[]))) + ;
			   		 [a.Emps = b.CEmps And ] + ;
			   		 Iif(Empty(lcGru), [], [b.Grupo = '] + lcGru + [' And ]) + ; 
			   		 Iif(Empty(lcCla), [], [b.Class = '] + lcCla + [' And ]) + ; 
			   		 [a.EmpDopNums ] + Iif(llNPr, [Not ], []) + ;
			   		 [In (Select b.EmpDopNums ] + ;
		 	  		  	   [From SigPcOoL b ] + ;
		 	  		  	  [Where b.Tipos = 'SIGPCOOP  ') ] + ;
			   [Order By a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Usuars, a.ContaOs, a.ContaDs, a.EmpDs]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csDados2') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csDados2)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select csDados2
	Scan
		If Not Seek(csDados2.Emps + csDados2.Dopes + Str(csDados2.Numes, 6), [csDados], [EmpDopNums])
			Insert Into csDados (Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Valos, Notas, Usuars, ContaOs, ContaDs, EmpDs) ;
						 Values (csDados2.Emps, csDados2.Dopes, csDados2.Numes, Ttod(Nvl(csDados2.Datas, Ctot(''))), ;
						 		 csDados2.Valos, csDados2.Notas, csDados2.Usuars, csDados2.ContaOs, csDados2.ContaDs, ;
						 		 csDados2.EmpDs)
		EndIf
	EndScan

	If llDel
		lcQuery = [Select a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Usuars, a.ContaOs, a.ContaDs, a.EmpDs ] + ;
					[From SigExMvc a, SigCdEmp b ] + ;
				   [Where a.Datas BetWeen ?pDtI And ?pDtF And ] + ;
				   		 [a.Dopes = '] + lcOpe + [' And ] + ;
				   		 Iif(Empty(lcEmp), [], [a.Emps = ']  + lcEmp + [' And ]) + ;
				   		 Iif(Empty(lcDes), [], [a.EmpDs = '] + lcDes + [' And ]) + ;
				   		 Iif(lnDoc=1, [Not a.Notas = '      ' And ], Iif(lnDoc=2, [a.Notas = '      ' And ], [])) + ;
				   		 [a.Emps = b.CEmps And ] + ;
				   		 Iif(Empty(lcGru), [], [b.Grupo = '] + lcGru + [' And ]) + ; 
				   		 Iif(Empty(lcCla), [], [b.Class = '] + lcCla + [' And ]) + ; 
				   		 [a.EmpDopNums ] + Iif(llNPr, [Not ], []) + ;
				   		 [In (Select b.EmpDopNums ] + ;
			 	  		  	   [From SigPcOoL b ] + ;
			 	  		  	  [Where b.Tipos = 'SIGPCOOP  ') ] + ;
				   [Order By a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Usuars, a.ContaOs, a.ContaDs, a.EmpDs]

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csDados2') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csDados2 - SigExMvc)')
			Return 0
		EndIf

		Select csDados2
		Scan
			If Not Seek(csDados2.Emps + csDados2.Dopes + Str(csDados2.Numes, 6), [csDados], [EmpDopNums])
				Insert Into csDados (Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Valos, Notas, Usuars, ContaOs, ContaDs, EmpDs, Deletado) ;
							 Values (csDados2.Emps, csDados2.Dopes, csDados2.Numes, Ttod(Nvl(csDados2.Datas, Ctot(''))), ;
							 		 csDados2.Valos, csDados2.Notas, csDados2.Usuars, csDados2.ContaOs, csDados2.ContaDs, ;
							 		 csDados2.EmpDs, .t.)
			EndIf
		EndScan
	Endif		
EndScan
oProg.Complete

ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdAcE', 'crSigCdAcE', 'Usuarios', Usuar, 'Emps')
Select crSigCdAcE
Index On Emps Tag Emps
If (Reccount('crSigCdAcE') > 0)
	Select csDados
	Scan
		If Not Seek(csDados.Emps, 'crSigCdAcE', 'Emps')	
			Delete In csDados
		EndIf
	EndScan
EndIf

If (RecCount('csDados') < 1)
	Go Top In csCpOI

	llLog = (crSigPcOop.Logs = 1) 			&& Copiar Arquivos de Log
	llApF = InList(crSigPcOop.Apagar, 2, 4) && Apagar Arquivos Fiscais da Origem

	ThisForm.CopiarFiscais

	* Verifica se Existem Todos os Diretórios de Destino

	llPcL = .f.
	If llLog
		ThisForm.poDataLogs   = CreateObject('fSqlConector', Alltrim(csDestinos.cIdConns), .t.)

		Select csDestinos
		oProg = CreateObject('fwprogressbar', 'Processando Diretórios de Log...', Reccount())
		oProg.Show
		Scan
			oProg.Update(.t.)

			With ThisForm.poDataLogs
				.AddCursor('SigOpLog', 'cIdChaves', 'DesLog')
			EndWith

			llAchouDes = ((Type('ThisForm.poDataLogs') = 'O') And Used('DesLog'))

			If llAchouDes  && Se Existe o Destino
				lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
						    [From SigOpLog ] + ;
						   [Where Copias = ?pNot]
						   
				If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csSigOpLogs') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csSigOpLogs)')
					Return 0
				EndIf

				If (Reccount('csSigOpLogs') > 0)
					Select csSigOpLogs
					Scan
						Select csSigOpLogs
						Scatter MemVar Memo
						m.cIdChaves = fUniqueIds()
					 	Insert Into DesLog From MemVar
					EndScan

					If (RecCount('csDestinos') = Recno('csDestinos'))
						If llApF && Apagar as Operações de Origem
							lcQuery = [Delete From SigOpLog Where Copias = ?pNot]
							If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
								=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Delete - SigOpLog)')
								Return 0
							EndIf
						Else
							lcQuery = [Update SigOpLog Set Copias = ?pTru Where Copias = ?pNot]
							If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, '') < 1)
								=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Update - SigOpLog)')
								Return 0
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
				EndIf
				
				llTransOk1 = .T.
				
				If (Reccount('DesLog') > 0)
					llPcL = .t.
					If Not ThisForm.poDataLogs.Update('DesLog')
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Update - DesLog)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
					Zap In DesLog
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					If (ThisForm.poDataLogs.Commit() < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Commit - 2)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If llTransOk1
					If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.Commit() < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Commit - 1)')
						llTransOk1 = .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf

				If Not llTransOk1
					ThisForm.poDataMgr.RollBack()
					ThisForm.poDataLogs.RollBack()
					Skip - 1 In csSelecionados
				EndIf
			Else
				=MessageBox('Diretório de Logs de Destino Não Encontrado!!!', 16, 'Contate o Suporte Técnico')
			EndIf
		EndScan
		oProg.Complete

		ThisForm.poDataLogs.Release
	EndIf
	
	If Not llPcL
		=Messagebox('Não Existem Dados Não Processados Com a Operação Selecionada Neste Período!!!', 0+48, '')
	Endif
	ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.SetFocus
	Return 0
EndIf

Select csDados
oProg = CreateObject('fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo as Operações...', Recc())
oProg.Show
Scan
	oProg.Update(.t.)
	Insert Into csOperacoes (SelImp, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Valos, Notas, Usuars, ContaOs, ContaDs, EmpDs, Deletado) ;
					 Values (.t., csDados.Emps, csDados.Dopes, csDados.Numes, csDados.Datas, csDados.Valos, ;
					 		 csDados.Notas, csDados.Usuars, csDados.ContaOs, csDados.ContaDs, csDados.EmpDs, csDados.Deletado)
EndScan
oProg.Complete

Use In csCpOI
Use In csDados
Use In csDados2
Set Talk Off

Select csOperacoes
Set Order To Operacao

Do Case
	Case lnOrd = 2 && PorData
		 Set Order To Data

	Case lnOrd = 3 && PorNota
		 Set Order To Nota

	Case lnOrd = 4 && PorOrigem
		 Set Order To Origem

	Case lnOrd = 5 && PorDestino
		 Set Order To Destino
EndCase

ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Option1.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Option2.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntFiltro.getEmpOs.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntFiltro.getDEmpOs.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntFiltro.getEmpDs.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntFiltro.getDEmpDs.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmdSelecionar.Enabled = .f.
ThisForm.cmdBtnForm.btnLimpar.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.cmdBtnForm.btnCopiar.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.cmdBtnGrade.btnMarcaTudo.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.cmdBtnGrade.btnDesmarcar.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.cmdBtnGrade.btnDocumentos.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.cntOperacoes.chkMensa.Enabled = .t.

Select csOperacoes
Go Top

ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Column1.SetFocus
ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Column1.Refresh
ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Refresh
* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
ThisForm.cntSombra.lblSombra.Caption = ThisForm.Caption
ThisForm.cntSombra.lblTitulo.Caption = ThisForm.Caption

=fConfigGeral()
With ThisForm
	.poDataMgr = CreateObject('fSqlConector', .Name, .t.)
	.poDataMgf = CreateObject('fSqlConector', .Name, .t.)

	.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Option1.Enabled = .t.
	.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Option2.Enabled = .t.
	.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Enabled 		= .t.
	.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.Enabled 		= .t.
	.cntFiltro.getEmpOs.Enabled  		= .t.
	.cntFiltro.getDEmpOs.Enabled 		= .t.
	.cntFiltro.getEmpDs.Enabled  	    = .t.
	.cntFiltro.getDEmpDs.Enabled 	    = .t.
	.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.Enabled  = .t.
	.cntOperacoes.cmdSelecionar.Enabled = .t.
	.cmdBtnForm.btnLimpar.Enabled       = .f.
	.cmdBtnForm.btnCopiar.Enabled       = .f.
	.cmdBtnGrade.btnMarcaTudo.Enabled   = .f.
	.cmdBtnGrade.btnDesmarcar.Enabled   = .f.
	.cmdBtnGrade.btnDocumentos.Enabled  = .f.
	.cntOperacoes.chkMensa.Enabled      = .f.
	.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.Value	= 1
	.cntOperacoes.getDtIni.Value		= Date()
	.cntOperacoes.getDtFin.Value 		= Date()

	If (.AcertaEmpDopNums() < 1)
		=Messagebox('A Chave EmpDopNums Está Inválida e Não Foi Possível Corrigir o Erro!!!' + Chr(13) + ;
					'Contate o Suporte Técnico e Informe Este Problema!!!', 0+48, 'Acerta EmpDopNums')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	If (.poDataMgr.pnIdconn < 1)
		=Messagebox('Impossivel Efetuar Conexão com Servidor de Banco de Dados...', 0+48, 'CreateObject')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	With ThisForm.Podatamgr
		.AddCursor('SigPcOnp', 'cIdChaves', 'CrSigPcOnp')
	EndWith

	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigPcOnp', 'LocalCopNp', .f., .f.)
	Select LocalCopNp
	Index On NoperOrigs Tag Nopers

	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPsg', 'crSigCdPsg', .f., .f.)
	Select crSigCdPsg
	Index On CGruCods Tag CGruCods

	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdGrp', 'crSigCdGrp', .f., .f., 'CGrus, CEstoqs, Mercs, DGrus, MontaGrDs, CjIcms, CodProds')
	Select crSigCdGrp
	Index On CGrus Tag CGrus

	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPac', 'crSigCdPac')
	.SigKey = crSigCdPac.SigKeys

	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPam', 'SigCdPam', .f., .f., 'TpInstalas, MascNums')
	.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPam', 'crSigCdPam', .f., .f., 'GrupoRecs, GrupoPags, ContaRecs, ContaPags, TpInstalas, ComGrupos, ComContas, MascNums')

	lcQuery = [Select Distinct Processos, Emps, Dopes, Codigos, cIdChaves ] + ;
				[From SigPcOoI ] + ;
			   [Order By Processos, Emps, Dopes, Codigos]

	If (.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csOrigens') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csOrigens)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select csOrigens
	Scan
		If Not fChecaAcesso('SIGCDPRC', Padl(csOrigens.Codigos, 10, '0'))
			Delete In csOrigens
		EndIf
	EndScan

	Select Distinct Processos ;
	  From csOrigens ;
	  Into Cursor csProcessos

	Select csProcessos
	Scan
		.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.AddItem(csProcessos.Processos)
	EndScan

	lcQuery = [Select b.Processos, b.Emps, b.Dopes, b.Codigos, b.EmpDs, b.DopeDs, b.TpProdutos, b.Dias, ] + ;
					 [b.GruconOs, b.GruconDs, b.EmpDests, b.Agrupar, b.NovoCods, b.CorTams, b.MoeVals, b.Franquias, ] + ;
					 [b.SemItens, b.Fiscais, b.PctMins, b.PctMaxs, b.Custos, b.Impostos, b.Barras, b.Mccrs, ] + ;
					 [b.Cheques, b.Delets, b.DesItens, b.MontaObs, b.ChkCons, b.MantemMats, b.MantDPros, b.Imagem, ] + ;
					 [b.MantProds, b.GdeGrupos, b.Origems, b.Destinos, b.CUnis, b.OriCompos, b.Drives as LocalDest, ] + ;
					 [c.Drives, c.cIdConns, b.Recalculos, b.PctCopias, b.OpItens, b.MovMZeros, b.MovCParc, b.cIdChaves, b.PctComps, b.Globalizas ] + ;
				[From SigPcOoI b, SigCdLoc c ] + ;
			   [Where b.Drives = c.Cods ] + ;
			   [Order By b.cIdChaves, b.Processos, b.Codigos]

	If (.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTodosDestinos') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTodosDestinos)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	ThisForm.cntOperacoes.optProcessados.Enabled = fChecaAcesso([SIGOPPOP],[OPJAPROC])
EndWith

**** Mena - 06/01/2011 - incluido pois dando erro ao selecionar ***
SELECT * from crTodosDestinos into cursor csdestinos 

With ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes
	.ColumnCount  	   = 11
	.RecordSource 	   = 'csOperacoes'
	.HeaderHeight 	   = 19
	.DeleteMark   	   = .f.
	.RecordMark   	   = .f.
	.ReadOnly     	   = .f.
	.Scrollbars   	   =  2
	.AllowHeaderSizing = .f.
	.AllowRowSizing    = .f.

	.Column1.ControlSource = 'csOperacoes.SelImp'
	.Column1.Movable   = .f.
	.Column1.Resizable = .f.
	.Column1.ReadOnly  = .f.
	.Column1.Sparse    = .f.
	.Column1.Check1.AutoSize = .t.

	.Column2.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Numes'
	.Column3.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Datas'
	.Column4.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Valos'
	.Column5.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Notas'
	.Column6.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Usuars'
	.Column7.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.ContaOs'
	.Column8.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.ContaDs'
	.Column9.ControlSource  = 'csOperacoes.Emps'
	.Column10.ControlSource = 'csOperacoes.EmpDs'
	.Column11.ControlSource = 'csOperacoes.Dopes'

	For I=2 To .ColumnCount
		lcCol = Alltrim(Str(I))
		.Column&lcCol..FontName = 'Courier New'
		.Column&lcCol..Movable   = .f.
		.Column&lcCol..Resizable = .f.
		.Column&lcCol..ReadOnly  = .t.
	Next
	.SetAll([DynamicForeColor],[Iif(csOperacoes.Deletado, Rgb(255,0,0), Rgb(0,0,0))], [Column])
EndWith
* Fim ********************************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Load
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Load
=fConfigGeral()

Create Cursor csBaixas (Cpros c(14), Qtds n(8,2))
Index On Cpros Tag Cpros

Create Cursor csOperacoes (SelImp l(1), Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Datas d(8), Valos n(12,2), ;
						   Notas c(10), Usuars c(10), ContaOs c(10), ContaDs c(10), EmpDs c(3), Deletado l(1))

Index On Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) 				Tag Operacao
Index On Dtos(Datas) + Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) Tag Data
Index On Notas + Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) 		Tag Nota
Index On ContaOs + Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) 	Tag Origem
Index On ContaDs + Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes, 6) 	Tag Destino

=DoDefault()
* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Release
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Release
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Release
ThisForm.poDataMgf.Release

=DoDefault()
* Fim **************************************************************************************

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPOP.cmdBtnGrade
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 7
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Do Case
   Case This.Value = 1 And This.btnMarcaTudo.Enabled
		* Marca Tudo
		Select csOperacoes
		Replace All SelImp With .t.
		Go Top
		ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Refresh
		 
   Case This.Value = 2 And This.btnDesmarcar.Enabled
   		* Desmarcar
		Select csOperacoes
		Replace All SelImp With .f.
		Go Top
		ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Refresh
		 
   Case This.Value = 3 And This.btnDocumentos.Enabled
   		* Operações
   		If csOperacoes.Deletado
   			=MessageBox([Essa Operação Foi Excluída do Sistema!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
   		Else
			Do Form SigMvExp With csOperacoes.Dopes, 'C', csOperacoes.Numes, csOperacoes.Emps, .t.
		EndIf
EndCase
* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE KeyPress
LParameters nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

Local SelImp

If InList(nKeyCode, 13, 32)
	m.SelImp = (Not csOperacoes.SelImp)

	Select csOperacoes
	Gather Memvar Fields SelImp
	ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdOperacoes.Refresh

	NoDefault
EndIf
* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPOP.cmdBtnForm
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 58
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If (This.Value = 2) && Se Clicou em Copiar Faz a Checagem
	Select SelImp, Count(1) as Selecionados ;
	  From csOperacoes ;
	 Where SelImp ;
	 Group By SelImp ;
	  Into Cursor csSeles
	  
	If (csSeles.Selecionados < 1)
		=Messagebox('Nenhum Lançamento Foi Encontrado!!!', 0+48, '')
		ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.SetFocus
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf 

Do Case
	Case (This.Value = 1) And This.btnLimpar.Enabled
		 * Limpar Seleção
		 ThisForm.LimpaSelecao()
		 
	Case (This.Value = 2) And This.btnCopiar.Enabled
		 * Copiar
		lcPro = Padr(ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.ListItem(ThisForm.cntOperacoes.cmbOperacoes.ListIndex), 20)
		
		If Used([csConexoes]) && Não Tirar Essa Checagem
			Use In csConexoes
		EndIf
		
		Select Distinct Drives, cIdConns ;
		  From crTodosDestinos ;
		 Where Processos = lcPro And Not Empty(cIdConns) ;
		  Into Cursor csConexoes
		Go Top In csConexoes
		If Eof([csConexoes])
			=Messagebox('Não Foi Possível Encontrar as Conexões!!!', 0+48, 'csConexoes')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		Dimension loConexao(Reccount('csConexoes'), 3)
		* 1 - Conexão dos Arquivos de Destino
		* 2 - Conexão Para Gravar o Autoriza do Destino (Igual ao 1)
		* 3 - Caminho dos Arquivos .Dbf

		Select csConexoes && Criando As Conexões
		Scan
			loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 1) = CreateObject('fSqlConector', Alltrim(csConexoes.cIdConns), .t., .t.)
			loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 2) = CreateObject('fSqlConector', Alltrim(csConexoes.cIdConns), .t., .t.)
			loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 3) = Alltrim(csConexoes.Drives)

			If (loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 1).pnIdconn < 1)
				=Messagebox('Não Foi Possível Criar a Conexão [' + csConexoes.cIdConns + ']', 0+48, 'CreateObject 1')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			If (loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 2).pnIdconn < 1)
				=Messagebox('Não Foi Possível Criar a Conexão [' + csConexoes.cIdConns + ']', 0+48, 'CreateObject 2')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		EndScan

		If ThisForm.Processamento() < 0
			Select csConexoes && Fechando as Conexões
			Scan
				ThisForm.poDataMgr.Rollback
				loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 1).Rollback
				loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 2).Rollback
			EndScan
		EndIf

		Select csConexoes && Fechando as Conexões
		Scan
			loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 1).Release
			loConexao(Recno('csConexoes'), 2).Release
		EndScan
		 
	Case This.Value = 3
		 * Sair
		 ThisForm.Release
EndCase
* Fim ********************************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
ThisForm.Release
ENDPROC
